(for example, gratings, multilayers, photonic crystals or metasur-
page=12 block=49352512 (x=3 y=53) width=249.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
faces). The second step would then be to choose the right material 
page=12 block=49352512 (x=3 y=64) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
platform. This would require creating a wide database of known 
page=12 block=49352512 (x=3 y=74) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
optical materials together with their properties and their evolution

under changing temperature and other harsh environments. By 
page=12 block=49352512 (x=3 y=95) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
using the available databases on chosen material platform proper-
page=12 block=49352512 (x=3 y=106) width=249.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
ties, machine-learning-driven global optimization could then be 
page=12 block=49352512 (x=3 y=116) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
used as the third step to obtain ultimate-efficiency device designs 
page=12 block=49352512 (x=3 y=127) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
and to determine suitable fabrication (growth condition, doping 
page=12 block=49352512 (x=3 y=137) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
level, stoichiometry and so on) and integration schemes.
page=12 block=49352512 (x=3 y=148) width=208.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
Neuromorphic photonics. While the rapid advances in deep learn-
page=12 block=49441896 (x=3 y=169) width=249.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
ing  provide  radically new approaches to solve  photonic design 
page=12 block=49441896 (x=3 y=179) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
problems, this assistance is not one-way but interactive99. ‘Big data’, 
page=12 block=49441896 (x=3 y=189) width=251.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
the catalyst of deep learning, continues to revolutionize AI research 
page=12 block=49441896 (x=3 y=200) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
with record-breaking improvements in various domains, while at the 
page=12 block=49441896 (x=3 y=211) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
same time it causes an exponential increase in computational power 
page=12 block=49441896 (x=3 y=221) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
and energy consumption. At present, most deep-learning algorithms 
page=12 block=49441896 (x=3 y=232) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
are deployed in conventional computers with von Neumann archi-
page=12 block=49441896 (x=3 y=242) width=249.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
tecture, whose serial nature is an intrinsic barrier to efficient support 
page=12 block=49441896 (x=3 y=253) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
for neural networks. Even with some application-specific integrated 
page=12 block=49441896 (x=3 y=263) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
circuits that are deliberately optimized to run deep-learning models, 
page=12 block=49441896 (x=3 y=274) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
most industrial-grade models for practical applications still take a 
page=12 block=49441896 (x=3 y=284) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
formidable time and cost to train. Superior to their electronic coun-
page=12 block=49441896 (x=3 y=295) width=249.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
terpart in both speed and power consumption, photonic platforms 
page=12 block=49441896 (x=3 y=305) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
for deep learning, including nanophotonic scatterers100, integrated 
page=12 block=49441896 (x=3 y=315) width=251.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
silicon photonic chips101–103 and 3D-printed diffractive layers104, are 
page=12 block=49441896 (x=3 y=326) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
under active investigation.
page=12 block=49441896 (x=3 y=337) width=97.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
The tremendous acceleration of deep-learning models on an 
page=12 block=49441896 (x=14 y=347) width=240.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
optical platform depends on the capability of parallel signal process-
page=12 block=49441896 (x=3 y=358) width=249.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
ing of light, which makes matrix–vector multiplications in constant 
page=12 block=49441896 (x=3 y=368) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
time with respect to the matrix dimension, in contrast to the qua-
page=12 block=49441896 (x=3 y=379) width=249.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
dratic time complexity on a digital processor. Besides matrix mul-
page=12 block=49441896 (x=3 y=389) width=249.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
tiplication, nonlinear activation functions play a key role in ANNs, 
page=12 block=49441896 (x=3 y=400) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
enabling them to learn complex mappings when cascading multiple 
page=12 block=49441896 (x=3 y=410) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
linear layers.  Such nonlinear activation functions for an optical 
page=12 block=49441896 (x=3 y=421) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
platform can be realized by using saturable absorption of 2D mate-
page=12 block=49441896 (x=3 y=431) width=249.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
rials105, nonlinear electro-optic modulation in silicon106, or simply 
page=12 block=49441896 (x=3 y=441) width=251.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
an external digital processor101, with implementation in a fixed or 
page=12 block=49441896 (x=3 y=452) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
reprogrammable manner107.
page=12 block=49441896 (x=3 y=462) width=103.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
Instead  of  implementing  mathematical  operations  such  as 
page=12 block=49441896 (x=14 y=473) width=240.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4

cal components allow a biological neural  system to be mimicked

ter recognition104. Training and learning in the system can be imple-

mented in either a supervised or an unsupervised manner. These

neuromorphic photonic platforms can make use of the overwhelm-

ing advantages of light in speed and parallelism when processing 
page=12 block=49441896 (x=3 y=610) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
information. The interactions between new photonic structures and 
page=12 block=49441896 (x=3 y=620) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
deep learning may overcome the limitation of current computing

approaches  and systems, and potentially lead AI research to new 
page=12 block=49441896 (x=3 y=641) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
horizons. Indeed, it has been demonstrated that inverse-designed

metastructures can solve integral equations using electromagnetic

fields110. On the other side, wave physics can also be regarded as

tonic structures, some of which may be designed by deep learning,

page=12 block=49441896 (x=3 y=715) width=82.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
summary
page=12 block=692060352 (x=264 y=32) width=40.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=Whitney-Semibold wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
The concept of deep learning has gone far beyond being a compu-
page=12 block=47726247 (x=264 y=43) width=249.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
tational analogy of biological neural systems and has emerged as 
page=12 block=47726247 (x=264 y=53) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
a powerful tool that solves extremely complex problems by build-
page=12 block=47726247 (x=264 y=64) width=249.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
ing up multilevel abstraction of massive data. For the photonics 
page=12 block=47726247 (x=264 y=74) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
community, deep learning and other AI techniques are currently 
page=12 block=47726247 (x=264 y=85) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
transforming the areas of optical design, integration and measure-
page=12 block=47726247 (x=264 y=95) width=249.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
ments. Deep-learning techniques have already demonstrated their 
page=12 block=47726247 (x=264 y=106) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
tremendous potential for photonic structure design, optimization of 
page=12 block=47726247 (x=264 y=116) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
architecture, materials and entire optical systems, and will continue 
page=12 block=47726247 (x=264 y=127) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
to uncover new ways to unparalleled speed-up of optical measure-
page=12 block=47726247 (x=264 y=137) width=249.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
ments and even unlocking new optical effects.
page=12 block=47726247 (x=264 y=148) width=170.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
In this Review, we have surveyed various model structures for 
page=12 block=47726247 (x=275 y=158) width=240.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
photonic design, ranging from individual plasmonic nanoparticles 
page=12 block=47726247 (x=264 y=169) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
to metamaterials comprising an array of meta-atoms, and to inte-
page=12 block=47726247 (x=264 y=179) width=249.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
grated  photonic  devices. All of these  remarkable developments 
page=12 block=47726247 (x=264 y=190) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
have been demonstrated within the past few years, and further 
page=12 block=47726247 (x=264 y=200) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
advances are expected as researchers with different backgrounds 
page=12 block=47726247 (x=264 y=211) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
contribute to this emerging field. Deep learning and AI research-
page=12 block=47726247 (x=264 y=221) width=249.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
ers should team up with optical scientists to develop unorthodox, 
page=12 block=47726247 (x=264 y=232) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
physics-driven algorithms and networks that are not only robust, 
page=12 block=47726247 (x=264 y=242) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
generative and interpretable while using fewer data, but also pro-
page=12 block=47726247 (x=264 y=253) width=249.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
vide unconventional ways to realize unparalleled optical functional-
page=12 block=47726247 (x=264 y=263) width=249.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
ities. Such cross-disciplinary approaches merging AI, photonics and 
page=12 block=47726247 (x=264 y=274) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
materials platforms will allow for large-scale photonic designs with 
page=12 block=47726247 (x=264 y=284) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
unique functionalities as well as new methods for optical charac-
page=12 block=47726247 (x=264 y=295) width=249.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
terization, paving the way to high-speed super-resolution imaging, 
page=12 block=47726247 (x=264 y=305) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
real-time detection and manipulation, efficient energy conversion 
page=12 block=47726247 (x=264 y=316) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
systems and transformative advances in the area of quantum mea-
page=12 block=47726247 (x=264 y=326) width=249.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
surements and metrology. On this path, the photonics community 
page=12 block=47726247 (x=264 y=337) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
should ultimately build an ‘optical structures and materials genome’ 
page=12 block=47726247 (x=264 y=347) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
to construct a comprehensive dataset of photonic concepts, archi-
page=12 block=47726247 (x=264 y=358) width=249.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
tectures, components and photonic materials to enable hierarchical 
page=12 block=47726247 (x=264 y=368) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
machine-learning algorithms that could provide ultimate-efficiency 
page=12 block=47726247 (x=264 y=379) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
devices. This effort should be extended to realize all-optical plat-
page=12 block=47726247 (x=264 y=389) width=249.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
forms to perform deep learning and other AI algorithms at the 
page=12 block=47726247 (x=264 y=400) width=251.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=MinionPro-Regular wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
speed of light, ushering in an even brighter AI era.
